## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a clear route (capabilities, team, process, portfolio, results, appendix) with divider slides, a consistent grid and a restrained color system, and slides 12 and 13 carry the argument with clear headlines and readable charts. It is built as a reading and reference document for advisors, so the dense tables on slides 16, 17, 20 and 36 are mostly appropriate, but the dense and overlong fine print and small table text make several pages hard to scan. Weaker spots hurt use: slide 30 packs three charts, a table and a line chart into one page with a small axis scale and unlabeled bar groupings, slide 20 is a very small two-table page, and the contents page, the cover and divider slides are plain, so the deck reads as competent compliance-driven material rather than a crafted story.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the four-step colored chevron flow with matching column text on slide 12, the action-title headlines with consistent subtitle and as-of dates, and the divider slides with the stable footer.

**Most useful repair:** Cut slide 30 into two slides, enlarge the table and chart text on slides 20, 21 and 22, and shorten or shrink the footnote blocks that crowd slides 13 and 30.

**What would change the score:** I would add a short summary of the key results up front, trim the pages in the middle that repeat positioning data, and label the colored bars on slide 13 and slide 30 directly so readers do not rely on the legends.

_Confidence high. 38 of 38 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

This 3Q 2025 strategy review for the JPMorgan Equity Income separately managed account is a numbered 38-slide deck. It opens with a cover and table of contents, then a Capabilities section covering the firm's roughly $4T in assets under supervision, its global equities resources, and equity assets under management. An Expertise section introduces the portfolio management and research team with experience figures for each person. The Portfolio section presents the bottom-up investment philosophy and process, a performance versus volatility chart, sector positions, top holdings, portfolio characteristics and third-quarter trades. A Results section compares annualized and calendar-year performance against the Russell 1000 Value Index, followed by quarter and year-to-date attribution tables. The appendix adds drawdown analysis, dividend sustainability charts, performance expectations, rolling excess returns, biographies, a GIPS composite reference, a glossary and disclosures. The deck closes on the disclosure page. Slide 13 pairs a percentile-ranking chart with a three-metric comparison, slide 30 uses three cumulative performance panels for market-cycle context, and slide 12 lays out the stock selection process as a four-step flow.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the J.P. Morgan Asset Management JPMorgan Equity Income strategy review deck have?**

The deck is a numbered 38-slide presentation, opening with a cover and table of contents on slides 1 and 2. Its sections are Capabilities, Expertise, Portfolio, Results, Appendix and GIPS Report, with the disclosure page closing the deck.

**What does the J.P. Morgan Asset Management deck say about its assets under supervision?**

Slide 4 is titled "JPMAM: Broad, diversified ~$4T AUS active manager" and shows total assets under supervision of $3.9T as of June 30, 2025. The same slide breaks these assets down by asset class, including Equities at $1,160 billion and Fixed Income at $925 billion.

**What annualized performance does J.P. Morgan Asset Management report for the Equity Income SMA Composite on slide 20?**

Slide 20 shows the Equity Income SMA Composite (Gross) at 5.46% for 3Q 2025 and 10.86% since inception (11/30/2002). Over the same periods the Russell 1000 Value Index shows 5.33% for 3Q 2025 and 9.16% since inception.

**How is the J.P. Morgan Asset Management stock selection process structured in the deck?**

Slide 12 lays out the bottom-up fundamental philosophy as a four-step flow: Idea generation, Fundamental analysis, Valuation analysis and Portfolio construction. The same slide states the portfolio holds 85-110 names with a maximum stock weighting of 5% at time of initiation.

**Layout**

Single slide with a title at top left (about 8.8% from the top) over a thin dark rule at about 13.4%. Below the rule, a row of five coloured number tiles spans the width, and a second row has the orange 37 tile at left and the green 48 tile at right, with a grey world map filling the centre between them. A row of four statistics with thin vertical dividers sits above the bold italic centred tagline at about 81% height, then a small footer note, a footer rule at about 89.8%, the page number 5 bottom left and the J.P.Morgan Asset Management logo bottom right. Left margin about 2.7%, right margin about 2.7%.

**Visuals**

Five coloured number tiles across the top row: 137 Fundamental Research in blue, 19 Quant. Research & Data Science in amber, 140 Portfolio Managers in green, 32 Traders & Analytics in purple, 55 Investment Specialists in navy. Second row: 37 Implementation in orange at left, 48 Sustainable Investing & Stewardship Team in olive green at right. Each tile has a large number at left and a label at right, separated by a thin vertical white line. Below each tile is a plain black list of sub-counts. A flat light grey (#E2E4E5) world map with white country borders fills the centre, with about nine small blue (#0092F9) ring markers with white centres at London, New York, Paris area, India, Hong Kong, Taiwan, Japan, Korea and Singapore. Four statistics with thin dark vertical dividers at about 28.9%, 54.9% and 77.5% of width. No photographs, charts or icons besides the logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles and body text black #000000. Typefaces: Arial (Helvetica-like grotesque sans-serif) regular for numbers and body, Arial Bold for the title, the statistic labels and the page number, Arial Bold Italic for the tagline. Title about 3.1% of slide height, body about 1.3% to 1.7%, big statistic numbers about 4.3%, tagline about 3.3%, footer about 1.4%. Tile colours: blue #0092F9, amber #FFBE37, green #00A16D, purple #6F44BB, navy #00458A, orange #EB7200, olive #7DA12E. Tile label text white except on amber where it is black. Tiles are flat rectangles with square corners, no shadows, no gradients. Title rule thin dark grey #313335. Map fill #E2E4E5.

**Layout**

Two equal-width donut charts sit side by side in columns separated by a vertical divider at the slide centre, under a full-width bold title at top left with a horizontal rule beneath it and a one-line subtitle. Text is left-aligned at a margin of about 3% of slide width; the source line sits above a thin footer rule near the bottom, with the J.P. Morgan wordmark bottom right and the slide number bottom left.

**Visuals**

Two donut charts, both with segments drawn clockwise from 12 o'clock. Left chart, titled Equities with total USD 1,260bn: a blue segment USD 893bn (71%) covering the right and bottom, a yellow segment USD 126bn (10%) at the lower left, and a green segment USD 242bn (19%) at the upper left. Legend below in three rows: blue square U.S. Equity, yellow square Emerging Markets & Asia Pacific Equity, green square International Equity Group. Right chart, titled U.S. Equity with total USD 893bn: a blue segment USD 407bn (46%) at the upper right and right, a yellow segment USD 162bn (18%) at the bottom, and a green segment USD 325bn (36%) covering the upper left and left. Legend in one row below: blue square Core, yellow square Value, green square Growth. Segments are separated by thin white gaps. Labels sit outside each segment with the USD value above and the percentage below in larger type. No highlighted points.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with black text. Title is a bold sans-serif (Arial/Helvetica style) in black, about 3.1% of slide height, with a thin black horizontal rule beneath it. Subtitle and body are regular sans-serif (Arial style) in black, about 2.6% of slide height; percentage labels about 3.7% and USD labels about 1.8%. Accents are blue #0092F9, yellow #FFBE37 and green #00A16D. A thin black vertical divider runs between the two chart columns. Shapes are flat with no shadows or gradients.

Expertise
7

**Layout**

The title 'Expertise' sits left-aligned at about 30% from the top, with thin white hairline rules across the full content width near the top (about 25.7% from top) and bottom (about 90% from top). The page number sits bottom-left and the J.P.Morgan Asset Management logo lockup sits bottom-right, both inside the bottom margin below the lower rule.

**Visuals**

none

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0092F9. Solid bright blue background #0092F9. Title in white (#FFFFFF) regular-weight geometric sans-serif resembling Helvetica/Arial, about 10.9% of slide height for the title, sentence case, no letter-spacing change. Page number in white bold sans-serif, about 1.4% of slide height. Two white hairline rules (about 1px at this resolution) spanning from about 2.7% to 97.3% of slide width. Logo in white: serif 'J.P.Morgan' wordmark over sans-serif capitals 'ASSET MANAGEMENT'. No shadows, gradients, or rounded corners.

**Text**

An experienced team dedicated to value

8

**Layout**

Single-column slide with a bold title at top-left above a full-width thin dark rule, then a one-line-wide intro subtitle under it. Below sits a light grey panel holding a staggered grid of circular headshots with name and role blocks: one centred row of two people, then a section label with a row of two, a row of three, and a section label with a row of four, each block separated by thin horizontal rules running through the row. Footnote line and rule sit at the bottom-left, the J.P.Morgan Asset Management logo bottom-right, and the page number bottom-left; left margin about 2.8% of width.

**Visuals**

Eleven circular headshot photographs, each cropped to a circle about 8% of slide width, with name and role text beside them. Subjects in order: Jon Sherman (man, short greying hair, navy suit, light blue open-collar shirt, grey studio backdrop); Don San Jose (smiling man, dark suit, white shirt, patterned tie, grey backdrop); Andrew Brandon (smiling man with glasses, dark suit, green tie, blurred city backdrop in blue-grey); David Silberman (smiling man with grey hair, dark suit, blue tie, blurred city backdrop); Lerone Vincent (smiling man, dark suit, blue striped tie, blurred blue-grey backdrop); Tony Lee (smiling man with round glasses, dark suit, grey tie, blurred backdrop); Laura Huang (smiling woman with long dark hair, dark top, blurred blue-grey backdrop); Jaime Steinhardt (smiling woman with brown hair, dark top, dark grey backdrop); Rory Houser (smiling man, navy suit, striped tie, blurred city backdrop); Anne Sisto (smiling woman with long brown hair, dark top, light grey backdrop); Ellie O’Donoghue (smiling woman with red hair, black top, dark grey backdrop). Lighting is soft and even, framing is head-and-shoulders, all photos are circular crops with no overlay. Thin black horizontal lines run behind the photos and name blocks. No charts, tables or logos apart from the footer logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a light grey #F5F7F8 rectangle behind the team grid. White slide background with a light grey #F5F7F8 panel behind the team grid. Titles are bold sans-serif (Arial Bold / Helvetica Bold) in black #000000, about 3.1% of slide height; subtitle is regular Arial in black #000000 at about 2.4%; section labels are Arial Bold black at about 2.2%; name lines are Arial Bold black at about 1.84%; role lines Arial Bold black at about 1.49%; experience lines Arial regular black at about 1.49%; footnote Arial regular at about 1.38%. Rules are thin dark grey (#333333) lines about 0.2% of height, title rule and footer rule are lighter grey. Headshots have no border or shadow. No corner radii on the panel, no gradients or shadows. The footer logo wordmark is a serif J.P.Morgan in black with a sans-serif ASSET MANAGEMENT line below.

9

**Layout**

Single full-width slide with the title left-aligned at the top-left margin (x 2.8%) above a thin black rule spanning the slide width, then an italic subtitle beneath it. Below sits a light grey panel holding a staff chart of 22 people arranged in rows: two centred at the top (David Small and Danielle Hines), then rows grouped by sector headings (Consumer and Healthcare; Financials and REITs; Telecom, Media & Technology; Industrials), each person with a circular photo followed by a name and role block, with a thin black horizontal rule running behind each row. A footnote sits bottom-left above a thin grey rule, the J.P.Morgan wordmark sits bottom-right, and the page number 9 sits bottom-left.

**Visuals**

Twenty-two circular portrait photographs of analysts, one per person, each a head-and-shoulders headshot in business attire against a soft, mostly neutral blurred background, lit evenly by front light, and cropped to a circle of about 6% of slide width. Each photo is placed to the left of its name block and sits over the row rule. Also a J.P.Morgan wordmark logo bottom-right with the words ASSET MANAGEMENT beneath it. Thin black horizontal rules: one under the title, one across the top row, one per staff row, and one above the footnote in grey. No charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a light grey panel #F5F7F8 behind the staff chart. White slide background with a very light grey panel (#F5F7F8) behind the staff area. Title and subtitle are black #000000 Arial Bold (title) and Arial Italic (subtitle), with the title about 3.1% of slide height and subtitle about 2.6%. Body text is black #000000 Arial regular, about 1.1% of slide height, with bold lines for names and sector/product names about 1.1 to 1.3%. Sector headings are black Arial Bold about 1.5%. Lines are black, about 0.2% of slide height thick. No shapes have rounded corners, shadows or gradients; photos are circular crops.

### Slide 10

**Text**

Portfolio / 10 / J.P.Morgan / ASSET MANAGEMENT

**Layout**

Single-title section divider with no grid: the title 'Portfolio' sits left-aligned at about 29.5% from the top, with thin white horizontal rules spanning the full content width near the top (y about 25.7%) and bottom (y about 89.8%). The page number sits bottom-left and the J.P.Morgan logo lockup sits bottom-right, both inside the bottom rule area with about 3% side margins.

**Visuals**

Thin white horizontal hairline rules at about y 25.7% (top) and y 89.8% (bottom), spanning x about 2.7% to 97.3%. J.P.Morgan wordmark logo (white serif 'J.P.Morgan' with 'ASSET MANAGEMENT' in white sans-serif capitals) at bottom right. No photographs, charts or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid blue #0092F9 fill covering the entire slide. Solid bright blue background #0092F9 with no gradient or texture. Title and page number and logo are white #FFFFFF. Title typeface is a regular-weight neo-grotesque sans-serif resembling Helvetica/Arial, about 10.9% of slide height, not capitalised. Page number is bold sans-serif about 1.4% of slide height. Hairline rules are white, about 1px thick at slide scale. No shapes, corner radii or shadows.

### Slide 11

**Text**

Investment philosophy
“Our bottom-up fundamental philosophy targets high quality U.S. companies
with attractive valuations and healthy dividend yields.”
1. Quality
We focus on quality first:
● Companies with durable franchises
● Consistent earnings
● Strong management teams
2. Valuation
Valuation is critical:
● Quantitative and qualitative judgements
● Potential vs. current market value
3. Dividend
Dividend yields:
● Enhance total return
● Generate income
● Modest payout ratio indicates a
disciplined use of capital
Our approach leads to a portfolio that has historically
shown less volatility than the market with less exposure to the downside
For illustrative purposes only. The manager seeks to achieve the stated objectives. There can be no guarantee the objectives will be met. See Glossary for index of investment terms.
J.P.Morgan
ASSET MANAGEMENT
11

**Layout**

Single-column content slide on a white ground with the title flush left at top and a thin dark rule beneath it spanning the margins (about 2.8% to 97.2% of width). Below the rule, a bordered quote box spans the full content width, followed by three equal columns (about 3% to 97% of width, each about 29% wide with 3% gutters) separated by thin vertical rules; each column has a coloured header bar, a down-pointing arrow, a bold lead-in and a bullet list. A full-width light grey conclusion panel sits below the columns, then a footnote line above a thin grey rule, with the J.P.Morgan Asset Management logo bottom-right and the page number bottom-left.

**Visuals**

No photographs, charts or tables. Three flat coloured header bars: a blue bar (#0092F9) with the white bold label '1. Quality', a yellow bar (#FFBE37) with the black bold label '2. Valuation', and a green bar (#00A16D) with the white bold label '3. Dividend'; each is a plain rectangle with square corners and no shadow. Below each bar is a solid black downward-pointing triangle about 3.2% of slide width wide and 2% tall, centred under its column. Two thin dark grey vertical divider lines (about #333333, roughly 0.1% of width) run from about 33.9% to 68.7% of slide height at x about 33.9% and 66.2%. A thin dark rule under the title spans the width, and a thin light grey rule sits above the footer. A light grey panel (#F5F7F8) holds the bold conclusion statement. A black-outlined rectangle (about 1 px, #000000, no fill, square corners) frames the quote. Logo: the 'J.P.Morgan' wordmark in a black serif typeface with 'ASSET MANAGEMENT' in black spaced capitals beneath it, bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the whole slide. White background. Title and quote text in black (#000000); column lead-ins and bullet text in near-black #101417; accent colours are the blue #0092F9, yellow #FFBE37 and green #00A16D headers, with the grey #F5F7F8 conclusion panel. Typefaces are Arial (Helvetica-style grotesque sans-serif): bold for the title, column headers, lead-ins and conclusion; regular for quote, bullets and footnote. Title is about 3.1% of slide height, column headers about 2.6%, lead-ins about 2.2%, bullets and quote about 2.0 to 2.6%, footnote and page number about 1.4%. Shapes have square corners, no gradients or shadows, and flat line styles.

### Slide 12

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Lays the investment process out as four left-to-right columns, each with a short header and bulleted criteria, so the sequence reads clearly at a glance.
- Review: The deck has a clear route (capabilities, team, process, portfolio, results, appendix) with divider slides, a consistent grid and a restrained color system, and slides 12 and 13 carry the argument with clear headlines and readable charts.
- Worth keeping: Keep the four-step colored chevron flow with matching column text on slide 12, the action-title headlines with consistent subtitle and as-of dates, and the divider slides with the stable footer.

**Text**

Bottom-up fundamental philosophy
Targeting high quality U.S. companies with attractive valuations and healthy dividend yields
Idea generation
Fundamental analysis
Valuation analysis
Portfolio construction
Narrow the investment universe via
● Analyst research
● Company meetings
● Industry conferences
Assess the quality of the company by analyzing
● Business factors
– Low cyclicality
– High barriers to entry
– Brand leadership and strength
● Management factors
– Disciplined use of capital
● Financial factors
– Sustainable cash flow
– Strong balance sheet
– Steady pattern of earnings
Valuation is critical to entry & exit points
● Metrics are tailored to each stock:
– Free cash flow yield
– P/E¹
– EV/EBITDA²
Sell discipline:
● Overvaluation by the market
● Displacement by a better idea
● Company fundamentals have changed
Constructed from the bottom-up
● Portfolio of 85-110 names with a maximum stock weighting of 5% at time of initiation
● Industry group constraints of benchmark weight +/-10%
● Position sizes are determined by:
– Strength of conviction
– Risk / reward opportunity
– Portfolio considerations of diversification
For illustrative purposes only. The manager seeks to achieve the stated objectives. There can be no guarantee the objectives will be met.
¹ Price/Earnings ² Enterprise value/Earnings before interest, taxes, depreciation, and amortization. See Glossary for index of investment terms.
J.P.Morgan
ASSET MANAGEMENT
12

**Layout**

Full-width title block at top left with a thin dark rule beneath it and a one-line subtitle below; a row of four chevron arrows spans the slide width, with four text columns beneath, each separated by a thin vertical rule. A grey callout sits in the lower part of the third column. Footnotes sit above a thin rule near the bottom, the J.P.Morgan logo is bottom right, and the page number is bottom left. Left and right margins are about 3%.

**Visuals**

Process diagram of four horizontal chevron arrows in a row, left to right: Idea generation (blue), Fundamental analysis (yellow), Valuation analysis (green), Portfolio construction (purple). Each chevron has a white gap to the next and the labels are centred in each chevron. Below the chevrons, four text columns of bullets, with thin vertical black dividers between columns 1-2, 2-3 and 3-4. A light grey rectangle (Sell discipline callout) sits at the bottom of column 3. Footer includes the J.P.Morgan wordmark and ASSET MANAGEMENT caption in the bottom right corner. No photographs, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles and headings in black Arial Bold (sans-serif, bold). Body text in black Arial Regular. Accents are the four chevron colours: blue #0092F9, yellow #FFBE37, green #00A16D, purple #6F44BB; white text on blue, green and purple, black text on yellow. Main title about 3.1% of slide height, subtitle about 2.4%, column headings about 2.2%, body and bullets about 1.84%, footnotes about 1.38%. Chevrons are flat-filled with sharp corners, no outlines and no shadows. Bullets are solid black dots, sub-bullets are en dashes. Grey callout #E2E4E5 with no border.

### Slide 13

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Places the strategy's percentile rankings for volatility, consistency and downside capture side by side with the median and index, making three comparisons readable at once.
- Review: The deck has a clear route (capabilities, team, process, portfolio, results, appendix) with divider slides, a consistent grid and a restrained color system, and slides 12 and 13 carry the argument with clear headlines and readable charts.
- Suggested repair: Cut slide 30 into two slides, enlarge the table and chart text on slides 20, 21 and 22, and shorten or shrink the footnote blocks that crowd slides 13 and 30.
- What would change the score: I would add a short summary of the key results up front, trim the pages in the middle that repeat positioning data, and label the colored bars on slide 13 and slide 30 directly so readers do not rely on the legends.

Source: J.P. Morgan Asset Management, eVestment. *Strategy Inception: 11/30/2002. Supplemental to standardized performance. Past performance is no guarantee of future results.
Risk is measured by standard deviation – a gauge of the variance of a manager's return over its average or mean. ** Down capture measures performance of the manager relative to the index in down markets. ***
Consistency is measured by batting average – calculated by dividing the number of months in which the manager beats or matches the index by the total number of months in the period.
Sourced from eVestment Alliance as of 6/30/2025. Statistics are computed using monthly data. The eVestment ranking is a percentile ranking based on the strategy's annualized return (gross of fees). Equity Income was ranked against the following number of institutional products in the eVestment Large Cap Value, for the since inception excess return (23rd percentile / 172 observations); standard deviation (9th percentile / 178 observations), batting average (8th percentile/ 172 observations), and down capture (13th / 172 observations). See Glossary for index of investment terms.

J.P.Morgan
ASSET MANAGEMENT

**Layout**

Two-column grid of four framed chart panels: left column holds Standard deviation (top) and Down capture (bottom); right column holds Batting average (top) and Performance (Net of Fee) (bottom), separated by a thin vertical divider at about x 50%. The title sits top-left at about y 9%, with a thin full-width rule beneath it and a subtitle below the rule at about y 15%. Left-aligned margins at about x 2.8% and right margin near 97%. Three-line source and footnote block runs across the bottom above a J.P.Morgan wordmark at bottom right; the slide number 13 sits bottom left at about y 95.7%.

**Visuals**

Four bar charts in panels. (1) Standard deviation since inception*: vertical bar chart, no axis scale printed, three bars with labelled values: JPMorgan Equity Income Strategy 13.2% (blue #0092F9), eVestment LCV Median 15.2% (yellow #FFBE37), Russell 1000 Value Index 15.2% (green #00A16D); callout 'Lower volatility: 9th percentile standard deviation' above the first bar. (2) Down capture potential since inception**: vertical bar chart with two bars: J.P. Morgan Equity Income Strategy 83% (blue), eVestment LCV Median 96% (yellow); callout 'Attractive potential downside protection / 13th percentile downside capture' above the left bar. (3) Batting average since inception***: vertical bar chart with two bars: J.P. Morgan Equity Income Strategy 57% (blue), eVestment LCV Median 52% (yellow); callout 'Alpha consistency: 8th percentile batting average' above right bar. (4) Performance (Net of Fee)*: clustered column chart around a zero baseline with no axis scale printed; legend top right: Equity Income (blue square), Russell 1000 Value Index (green square). Categories Down Quarter (Equity Income -5.5%, Russell 1000 Value Index -7.1%), Down Year (Equity Income -7.2%, Russell 1000 Value Index -11.3%), Since Inception (Equity Income 10.7%, Russell 1000 Value Index 9.9%). Callout 'Better Performance / 23rd percentile returns' at top left. No highlighted points beyond the callouts. Bars in charts 1 and 2 are not highlighted apart from colour coding. Logo: J.P.Morgan wordmark in serif with ASSET MANAGEMENT in sans-serif caps below, bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background, no gradient. Titles and headings in black #000000 Arial Bold sans-serif. Body and labels in black #000000 or dark slate #21292E Arial regular. Accents: blue #0092F9, yellow #FFBE37, green #00A16D. Panel headers are light grey #F5F7F8 filled bars with bold black text. Title size about 3.1% of slide height (roughly 6-7% cap height), body and labels about 1.5%, footnotes about 1.3%. Bars are flat with no corner radius, no shadows, no outlines. Thin black axis baselines about 0.2% height. Vertical divider and title rule are thin dark grey lines.

ASSET MANAGEMENT

**Layout**

Single-slide chart layout on a white page with generous margins (left edge about 3% of width). The title Sector positions sits top-left at about 9% from the top, with a thin dark horizontal rule beneath it spanning the full content width. The subtitle sits left under the rule, with a small Benchmark label box at the top right. The chart occupies the middle band with the bars left-to-right and a rule-separated data table directly beneath the category labels. Source footnote is left-aligned below the table, followed by a footer rule, the J.P.Morgan logo bottom right and the page number bottom left.

**Visuals**

Vertical bar chart titled Absolute sector & relative weightings (%). Y axis runs 0 to 30 in steps of 5, unit percent (%), with tick labels at 0, 5, 10, 15, 20, 25 and 30. X axis lists 11 sectors as categories. One series of solid blue bars, each with a value label above it: Financials 26.6; Industrials 14.9; Health Care 13.5; Information Technology 11.3; Consumer Discretionary 8.0; Consumer Staples 7.6; Energy 5.3; Utilities 4.7; Real Estate 3.1; Materials 2.4; Communication Services 1.7. No legend; no highlighted points. Beneath the chart is a table with one row labelled Relative Positioning (%)1 and values in the same column order: 3.9, 1.8, 1.8, 0.9, 0.3, 0.0, -0.7, 0.2, -1.1, -1.7, -6.3. Logo: J.P.Morgan wordmark with ASSET MANAGEMENT beneath, bottom right. No photographs or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with no texture. Titles and body text are black #000000. Accent colour for bars is bright blue #0092F9; the table cells are very pale grey #F5F7F8 and the Benchmark label cell is light grey #E4E6E8 with a white value cell. Title Sector positions is bold sans-serif (Arial Bold style), about 3.1% of slide height. Subtitle and body text are regular Arial-style sans-serif, about 1.7% to 2.4%; axis labels about 1.7%; category labels about 1.6%; footnote about 1.4%. Rules are thin dark grey lines about 0.1% of height. No corner radii, shadows or gradients. Bars are flat with no outlines. The J.P.Morgan wordmark is a serif face in black.

**Layout**

Two columns below a full-width title block: a light grey panel on the left (x 2.8 to 31.4%, y 25.8 to 78.6%) holding the key figure and caption, and a donut chart with a vertical legend on the right (chart about x 38 to 69%, legend x 72.7 to 93.5%). Title sits top-left at y 8.8% above a full-width thin dark rule at about y 13.4%; subtitle below it at y 15.2%. Left margin 2.8%, all text left-aligned. Source line at y 87.8% above a thin grey rule at about y 91.6%; J.P.Morgan wordmark bottom-right and page number 15 bottom-left.

**Visuals**

Donut chart, untitled, with a percentage label outside each segment and a legend at right. Segments run clockwise from 12 o'clock: Diversified Banks 8.3% (bright blue #0092F9, largest segment, about 8.3% of the ring), Investment Banking & Brokerage 5.2% (amber #FFBE37), Asset Management & Custody Banks 4.3% (orange #EB7200), Consumer Finance 3.6% (green #00A16D), Other Insurance 2.4% (dark navy #00458A), Insurance Brokers 1.2% (purple about #7030A0), Transaction & Payment Services 0.9% (olive green about #7FA332), Financial Exchanges & Data 0.7% (red #FF0000). The eight values sum to 26.6%, matching the panel figure. Ring is about 15% of slide width in outer radius with a white hole of about half the outer radius; segments separated by no visible gaps. No axes, no legend title, no highlighted points. Legend: eight rows of small filled squares (about 1% of slide width) in the segment colours, each followed by its category name, spaced about 3.6% apart vertically from y 34.2% to 58.8%. Left grey panel is a flat rectangle with no photograph or icon.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the full slide. Plain white background with no gradients or shadows. Titles and figure in black #000000 Arial Bold for the title and Arial Regular for the 26.6% figure; caption subtitle in Arial Bold #000000; body text 'Portfolio diversification across sub-sector' in Arial Regular #101417. Title about 3.1% of slide height, subtitle and labels about 1.8%, panel figure about 6.9%, body about 2.7%, source line about 1.4%. Accent colours are the chart palette: blue #0092F9, amber #FFBE37, orange #EB7200, green #00A16D, navy #00458A, purple, olive, red. Thin dark rule #333333 under title, thin grey rule #BBBBBB above footer. Grey panel fill #F5F7F8, square corners, no border. Chart segments are flat fills.

Three tables, no charts, photographs or logos apart from the wordmark. **Layout**

Single slide with a bold title top-left at about 8.8% of height, a thin dark rule beneath it spanning the full content width, and a grey subtitle line below on the left. The content sits in two columns: a data table occupies the left column (x 3 to 48%), while the right column (x 52 to 97%) holds a horizontal bar chart on top and a column chart below, separated by a horizontal rule and a vertical divider at x 50%; a benchmark selector box sits top-right, the source footnotes sit bottom-left above a footer rule, the J.P.Morgan logo sits bottom-right, and the page number 17 sits bottom-left.

**Visuals**

Three visual elements. (1) Table: 3 columns (row label, Portfolio, Benchmark) and 13 rows (header plus 12 data rows) with values: P/E Ratio 16.8x / 17.1x; EPS Growth 10.0% / 9.7%; Average Market Cap $258.0 B / $518.2 B; Number of Holdings 83 / 856; Current Dividend Yield 2.3% / 1.5%; Dividend Growth 4.9% / 6.1%; Active Share 68.3% / N/A; ROE 20.0% / 19.2%; ROIC 11.7% / 10.7%; Standard Deviation 14.6% / 15.8%; Turnover 15.7% / N/A; Tracking Error 2.67 / N/A; Beta 0.91 / 1.00. Header row is grey #E2E4E5 with bold labels. (2) Horizontal bar chart titled BARRA tilts relative to benchmark, with a vertical black zero axis at about x 76.7%; no axis scale or units are printed. Bars are labelled with values: Size 0.22 (blue), Dividend Yield 0.21 (blue), Leverage 0.18 (blue), Earnings Quality 0.07 (blue), Momentum 0.07 (blue), Earnings Yield 0.01 (blue), Growth -0.02 (yellow-orange), Profitability -0.06 (yellow-orange), Value -0.10 (yellow-orange), Beta -0.14 (yellow-orange). Positive bars extend right of the axis in blue #0092F9, negative bars extend left in yellow #FFBE37. No legend and no highlighted points. (3) Column chart titled Market capitalization weightings with superscript 3, legend Portfolio (blue #0092F9) and Benchmark (yellow #FFBE37), no y-axis or scale printed, black baseline. Categories and labelled values: > $25 B Portfolio 98%, Benchmark 85%; $10 B - $25 B Portfolio 1%, Benchmark 11%; $5 B - $ 10 B Benchmark 4% with no visible Portfolio bar (about 0%); < $5 B label 1% with no visible bar, series not identifiable from position. No photographs, icons or illustrations.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF covering the full slide. Clean white corporate financial slide in Arial-style sans-serif. Titles and body text are black #000000; accents are blue #0092F9 and yellow-orange #FFBE37 for chart series, with grey #E2E4E5 and #F5F7F8 table fills and dark grey #333333 rules. Title is Arial Bold at about 3.1% of slide height; subtitle and body text are Arial Regular at about 2.4% and 1.7% respectively; chart headings are Arial Bold at about 2.2%; footnotes at about 1.4%; page number Arial Bold at about 1.4%. Shapes are square-cornered with no shadows or gradients; table row separators are thin light grey lines.

18

**Layout**

Two equal columns split by a thin vertical divider at the slide centre (x about 50%, running from about y 19.5% to 75.5%). The title sits top-left at about 8.8% from the top with a thin full-width rule beneath it at about 13.4%, then a subtitle line at about 15.2%. Each column has an icon, a bold heading, and five list rows separated by thin rules, with the left margin about 2.8% and the right margin about 2.8%. A footnote block sits bottom-left above a thin footer rule at about 89.8%; the J.P. Morgan logo sits bottom-right and the page number 18 sits bottom-left at about 95.7%.

**Visuals**

Two small line-and-colour icons. (1) Top-left above Top buys: a stack of three outlined coins drawn in thin black strokes with a solid bright blue circle bearing a white dollar sign overlapping their lower-left, icon about 4.2% wide by 5.4% high, unboxed on white. (2) Top-right above Top sells: a black-outlined open hand palm-up holding a solid bright blue circle with a white dollar sign, icon about 4.1% wide by 5.4% high, unboxed on white. Ten round chevron buttons, each a light grey circle (#F2F3F5) about 3% wide by 4% high with a bold black right-pointing chevron, one beside each list item: five in the left column at about x 3.0% and five in the right column at about x 52.0%, vertically centred on each row. Thin black horizontal rules (about 0.1% of slide height) separate the rows, four per column. No chart, table or photograph.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background (#FFFFFF) with no gradient, texture or shadow. Titles and headings are black (#000000) bold Arial-style sans-serif (Helvetica/Arial Bold), title about 3.1% of slide height, column headings about 3.0%. Body and list text is black (#000000) regular Arial-style sans-serif about 2.2% of slide height. Footnotes are black regular sans-serif about 1.3% of slide height, with Glossary in bold. Page number is bold about 1.4%. Accent colour is bright blue (#0096FF) on the coin and dollar icons. Chevron buttons use light grey (#F2F3F5) with black chevrons. Rules are black (#000000) hairlines; the footer rule is grey (#8C8C8C). Corners are square apart from the circular chevron buttons. No shadows.

### Slide 20

**Review notes**

- Review: It is built as a reading and reference document for advisors, so the dense tables on slides 16, 17, 20 and 36 are mostly appropriate, but the dense and overlong fine print and small table text make several pages hard to scan.
- Review: Weaker spots hurt use: slide 30 packs three charts, a table and a line chart into one page with a small axis scale and unlabeled bar groupings, slide 20 is a very small two-table page, and the contents page, the cover and divider slides are plain, so the deck reads as competent compliance-driven material rather than a crafted story.
- Suggested repair: Cut slide 30 into two slides, enlarge the table and chart text on slides 20, 21 and 22, and shorten or shrink the footnote blocks that crowd slides 13 and 30.

Please note, the “net of fee” composite performance returns is calculated using a model investment management fee. It is based on a representative fee applicable to institutional clients looking to invest in the strategy and it is higher or equal to the weighted average investment management fee of the underlying accounts within the composite at year end. Actual fees may be lower based on assets under management and other factors. Where fees are lower, “net of fees” performance returns will be higher. As such, “net of fees” performance for actual accounts may differ significantly from the “net of fees” performance shown above. Excess returns are calculated by subtracting the benchmark returns from the strategy returns. Source: J.P. Morgan Asset Management. **Layout**

Single full-width content column on a white slide with a 2.8% left margin and roughly 2.7% right margin. The title 'Performance' sits top-left at about 8.8% from the top, followed by a thin black full-width rule at about 13.3%, then the subtitle line; two stacked tables (market value and annualized/calendar performance) fill the middle, the disclaimer paragraph sits in the lower band from about 82.8% to 91.5%, the J.P.Morgan logo is bottom-right, and the page number '20' is bottom-left at about 95.7%.

**Visuals**

Two data tables, no photographs, charts or icons. Table 1 (Market value): header row with columns 'Market value' and 'As of 9/2025'; one row 'Equity Income Strategy' with value '$76,606 mm'. Table 2 (Annualized performance): columns 'Annualized performance (%)', '3Q 2025', 'YTD 2025', '1 year', '3 years', '5 years', '10 years', 'Since Inception (11/30/2002)'; rows: Equity Income SMA Composite (Gross) 5.46, 12.04, 9.89, 14.66, 13.48, 11.87, 10.86; Equity Income SMA Composite (Net of max. allowable fees – 300bps)* 4.66, 9.51, 6.59, 11.22, 10.07, 8.52, 7.53; Russell 1000 Value Index 5.33, 11.65, 9.44, 16.96, 13.88, 10.72, 9.16; Excess Returns (Gross) 0.13, 0.39, 0.45, -2.30, -0.40, 1.15, 1.70 (bold); Excess Returns (Net) -0.67, -2.15, -2.85, -5.74, -3.80, -2.20, -1.63 (bold). Table 3 (Calendar year performance): header 'Calendar year performance (%)' then 2010 to 2024 as 15 columns; rows: Equity Income SMA Composite (Gross) 19.31, 9.08, 14.22, 32.13, 14.60, -1.14, 15.94, 18.29, -4.00, 27.24, 4.20, 26.25, -1.23, 5.33, 13.02; Equity Income SMA Composite (Net of max. allowable fees – 300bps)* 15.91, 5.90, 10.94, 28.47, 11.31, -4.08, 12.62, 14.93, -6.88, 23.42, 1.10, 22.46, -4.19, 2.17, 9.63; Russell 1000 Value Index 15.51, 0.39, 17.51, 32.53, 13.45, -3.83, 17.34, 13.66, -8.27, 26.54, 2.80, 25.16, -7.53, 11.46, 14.37; Excess Returns (Gross) 3.80, 8.69, -3.29, -0.40, 1.15, 2.69, -1.40, 4.63, 4.27, 0.70, 1.40, 1.09, 6.30, -6.13, -1.35 (bold); Excess Returns (Net) 0.40, 5.51, -6.57, -4.06, -2.14, -0.25, -4.72, 1.27, 1.39, -3.12, -1.70, -2.70, 3.34, -9.29, -4.74 (bold). **Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title and body text in near-black #000000 with table labels in #101417. Accent colour is a bright blue #0092F9 used for table header bars and the market value header. Typefaces are Arial (sans-serif) regular and bold; title is bold at about 3.1% of slide height, subtitle regular about 2.4%, table text about 1.7% to 1.8%, disclaimer about 1.3%. Light grey #F5F7F8 band fills for the two Excess Returns rows in each table. Thin grey horizontal dividers between table rows. Square corners, no shadows, no gradients.

21

**Layout**

Single white slide on a 16:9-like canvas with a left margin of about 3% and right margin of about 3%. The bold title sits top-left at about 9% from the top, with a thin dark rule beneath it spanning the width. Below, a two-column band holds the Top contributors table (left, blue header) and the Top detractors table (right, grey header), with the benchmark box and stock/sector box stacked top-right above them. A full-width sector attribution bar chart with its legend top-right fills the lower middle, followed by a footnote paragraph, a J.P.Morgan logo bottom-right and the page number bottom-left.

**Visuals**

Two data tables and one clustered column chart, plus a small benchmark label box, a stock/sector summary box, a J.P.Morgan wordmark logo, and divider rules. Top contributors table: columns Stock / Relative weight (%) / Impact (%); rows Seagate Technologies 1.08 0.55; Corning 0.90 0.42; AbbVie 1.25 0.30; Norfolk Southern 1.56 0.28; General Dynamics 1.55 0.25. Top detractors table: columns Stock / Relative weight (%) / Impact (%); rows Alphabet* -2.75 -0.91; JPMorgan Chase & Co¹* -2.91 -0.26; Fidelity National Information Services 0.83 -0.18; Intel* -0.37 -0.17; Micron Technology* -0.50 -0.17. Chart: clustered vertical bar chart titled Sector attribution (%), y-axis unlabelled with no printed scale (zero baseline is a black horizontal line), values printed above or below each bar, no axis ticks. Two series: Stock Selection in blue #0092F9 and Sector Allocation in amber #FFBE37, legend top-right of chart. Printed values by category (stock, sector): Information Technology 0.9%, 0.0%; Consumer Discretionary 0.2%, 0.0%; Health Care 0.2%, 0.0%; Utilities 0.1%, 0.0%; Financials 0.1%, 0.0%; Industrials 0.0%, 0.0%; Real Estate -0.1%, 0.0%; Consumer Staples -0.1%, 0.0%; Energy -0.1%, 0.0%; Materials -0.1%, 0.0%; Communication Services -0.4%, -0.3%. No highlighted points. Category labels sit under the zero line, two-line wrapped where needed. The J.P.Morgan logo is a serif wordmark with ASSET MANAGEMENT in sans-serif capitals beneath. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate financial style on white. Title is bold black sans-serif (Arial Bold / Helvetica Bold) at about 3.1% of slide height. Subtitle is regular black Arial about 2.4% of height. Table text and chart labels are regular black Arial about 1.8% and 1.7%. Footnote is Arial about 1.5% with bold italic lead-in. Accent blue #0092F9 used for the contributors header, stock selection bars and the upper blue box; amber #FFBE37 for sector allocation bars; light grey #E2E4E5 for the detractors header and benchmark label cell; zebra row tint #F5F7F8; pale cyan #DBF5F7 for the stock/sector box. Thin dark rules #3C3C3C about 0.2% of height. No rounded corners, shadows or gradients. Table rows separated by thin light grey lines.

**Layout**

Single white slide with a bold title at top left above a full-width dark rule, a subtitle line beneath it, and a benchmark tag box at top right. Two side-by-side tables (top contributors left, top detractors right) sit in the upper half above a rule, followed by a full-width sector attribution bar chart with category labels, a footnote block, a page number bottom left and the J.P. Morgan wordmark bottom right; left margin about 3%.

**Visuals**

Two data tables and one clustered column chart. Left table: Top contributors, columns Relative weight (%) and Impact (%), header fill blue #0092F9 with white bold text, rows Seagate Technologies 0.96 / 1.21; Wells Fargo 2.66 / 0.62; Charles Schwab 1.75 / 0.56; Corning 0.70 / 0.54; CVS 1.03 / 0.53. Right table: Top detractors, same columns, header fill light grey #E2E4E5 with black bold text, rows JPMorgan Chase & Co1* -2.89 / -0.95; Alphabet* -0.94 / -0.88; UnitedHealth 0.68 / -0.56; Micron Technology* -0.45 / -0.37; Berkshire Hathaway* -3.49 / -0.36. Rows alternate white and #F5F7F8. Chart: clustered column chart titled Sector attribution (%), no axis lines or gridlines shown, data labels printed to one decimal place in percent. Series Stock Selection (blue #0092F9) and Sector Allocation (orange #FFBE37), legend top right. Values by sector, stock selection then sector allocation: Information Technology 1.8 / 0.1; Consumer Staples 0.5 / 0.0; Health Care 0.6 / -0.1; Financials 0.1 / 0.1; Consumer Discretionary 0.1 / -0.1; Real Estate -0.1 / 0.1; Industrials -0.2 / 0.0; Utilities -0.2 / 0.0; Materials -0.3 / 0.0; Energy -0.3 / 0.0; Communication Services -0.7 / -0.5. Zero baseline is a black horizontal line. No point is highlighted. Small cyan box shows Stock: 1.38% | Sector: -0.53%. Logo: J.P.Morgan serif wordmark with ASSET MANAGEMENT caption in bottom right corner.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background, clean corporate financial look. Title in bold Arial-style sans-serif black #000000 about 3.1% of slide height; subtitle regular sans #000000 about 2.4%; table and chart text regular sans about 1.7% to 1.8%; footnotes about 1.5%. Accent blue #0092F9 for table header and stock selection bars; orange #FFBE37 for sector allocation bars; light grey #E2E4E5 header, #F5F7F8 row stripe, pale cyan #DBF5F7 summary box. Thin dark grey rules #333333 about 0.2% high separate header from body. No corner radii, shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Three equal columns separated by thin vertical black rules at about 3%, 35% and 67% of the slide width, running from about 19.5% to 77% of the height, with the title left-aligned at top-left (margin about 2.8%) above a full-width horizontal rule at about 13.5%. Each column holds an icon, a bold heading, a rule, a bold lead sentence and bullets; the italic disclaimer, source note, J.P.Morgan logo and page number 23 sit in the bottom band below a thin rule at about 90% of the height, with the page number at bottom-left and the logo at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Three blue flat icons, one above each column heading. Left: a people icon with one solid blue head and blue semicircle body in the centre, flanked by two outlined smaller figures with thin black outlines, occupying about 5.3% to 11.3% of width and 20.8% to 27.2% of height. Centre: a solid blue folder with a thin black outline on its tab and right edge, about 37.5% to 43.3% of width and 20.8% to 27.2% of height. Right: a black-outlined line chart frame (vertical and horizontal axis lines forming an L) with three solid blue bars of ascending height and a black arrow rising diagonally from the bottom-left to the upper-right, about 69.2% to 75.2% of width and 20.4% to 26.8% of height. No photographs, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Title and headings are black (#000000) bold Arial-style sans-serif; body and bullets are black regular Arial-style sans-serif. Title is about 3.1% of slide height, column headings about 3.0%, lead sentences about 2.2%, bullets and subtitle about 2.0% and 2.4%, source note about 1.5%. Accent colour is bright blue #0091FF used only in the three icons. Rules and column dividers are thin black lines (about 1 px) with no shadows, radii or gradients.

Appendix / 24 / J.P.Morgan / ASSET MANAGEMENT

**Layout**

Single-column section divider with a full-width thin black rule across the upper-middle of the slide, the title Appendix left-aligned beneath it at the left margin, and a thin grey footer rule near the bottom with the page number at bottom left and the J.P.Morgan ASSET MANAGEMENT logo lockup at bottom right, both aligned to the right margin; the upper and centre-right areas are empty white space.

**Visuals**

none. Apart from the rules, the logo lockup at bottom right is the only non-text graphic: the J.P.Morgan wordmark in a serif typeface with the words ASSET MANAGEMENT beneath it in small sans-serif capitals.

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Title and page number text in black #000000; the wordmark is black #000000. Title typeface is Arial regular, sans-serif, at about 10.9% of slide height. Page number is Arial bold at about 1.4% of slide height. Top rule is a black #000000 hairline about 0.1% of slide height spanning the margins; footer rule is a thin mid-grey hairline (#A0A0A0 approximate). No shapes, corner radii, shadows or gradients.

25

**Layout**

Single-column chart slide with a full-width headline at top-left (left edge about 3% of the width), a thin dark rule beneath it, and a full-width blue subtitle banner spanning about 2.6% to 97.2% of the width. The bar chart fills the middle of the slide with its legend above the plot at upper centre-right. The source note sits at the bottom left in two lines spanning about 3% to 96% of the width, the J.P.Morgan logo sits bottom right, and the page number 25 sits bottom left below the source note. Margins are about 3% on the left and right.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical bar chart, single series titled Excess return, legend marker a small blue square placed above the plot. Y-axis 0% to 15% with ticks at 0%, 5%, 10% and 15%, no axis title, tick labels in black Arial. X-axis is categorical with month labels May-08 through Sep-22 (20 categories), no axis title. Bars are solid blue #0092F9 with no outline, evenly spaced with narrow gaps. Value labels above each bar: May-08 2%, Jun-08 3%, Jul-08 3%, Aug-08 3%, Sep-08 5%, Oct-08 6%, Nov-08 7%, Dec-08 7%, Jan-09 9%, Feb-09 10%, Mar-09 9%, Apr-09 8%, May-09 6%, Jun-09 6%, Jul-09 5%, Aug-09 3%, Sep-09 0%, Mar-20 4%, Apr-20 3%, Sep-22 6%. Reference line: horizontal dashed orange line (about #E87722, stroke about 0.4% of slide height, dash length about 1.5% of width) at the 5.2% level, spanning from the Sep-08 bar area to the Sep-22 bar. Callout: rectangle with thin dashed orange border (#E87722) and no fill containing bold black text Average outperformance = (line break) 5.2%, placed in the upper right of the plot over the Jul-09 to Sep-22 area with its bottom edge just above the dashed line. Highlighted point: none beyond the dashed reference line. Bars are not cropped and no gridlines are shown.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white slide with no ornaments. Headline in bold Arial/Helvetica-style sans-serif, black #000000, size about 3.0% of slide height, left aligned. A thin dark grey rule (#404040, about 0.1% of height) runs under the headline. Subtitle in bold Arial-style sans-serif, white #FFFFFF on a solid blue #0092F9 band, size about 1.7% of slide height. Chart labels and footnote in regular Arial-style sans-serif, black #000000, size about 1.7% to 2.2% of slide height. Accent colours: blue #0092F9 bars and banner, orange #E87722 dashed lines and callout border. Shapes are square-cornered with no shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Three equal-width chart panels in columns separated by thin vertical rules at about 33.6% and 66.3% of slide width, under a full-width title at top-left with a thin horizontal rule beneath it and a subtitle line; a source footnote block sits above a thin footer rule, with the J.P. Morgan logo bottom-right and the page number bottom-left. Left margin about 2.8% and right margin about 2.7%, all text left-aligned.

**Visuals**

Three vertical bar charts side by side, each with a bold heading and a regular subtitle giving the measure. Chart 1 'Higher dividend yield', Gross Dividend Yield, y-axis 0% to 3% in 1% steps with light grey horizontal gridlines at each step and black axis lines; bars: Equity Income 2.3% (blue #0092F9, labelled 2.3%), Russell 1000 Value Index 1.5% (grey #C5C7C9, labelled 1.5%); category labels below the x-axis. Chart 2 'Consistent dividend growth', Trailing 12 Month Dividend Growth, y-axis 0% to 7% in 1% steps with light grey gridlines; bars: Equity Income 4.9% (amber #FFBE37, labelled 4.9%), Russell 1000 Value Index 6.1% (grey #C5C7C9, labelled 6.1%). Chart 3 'More dividend increases', Trailing 12 Month Dividend Increase**, y-axis 0% to 90% in 10% steps with light grey gridlines; bars: Equity Income 80% (green #00A16D, labelled 80%), Russell 1000 Value Index 52% (grey #C5C7C9, labelled 52%). No legend; each chart's categories are printed beneath its bars. No highlighted points beyond the coloured Equity Income bars. Logo: J.P.Morgan wordmark in serif type with ASSET MANAGEMENT in spaced capitals beneath.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with black text throughout. Titles in bold sans-serif (Arial Bold style) black #000000; subtitle and body in regular Arial/Helvetica style black #000000; chart subtitles in near-black #101417. Accent colours: blue #0092F9, amber #FFBE37, green #00A16D for Equity Income bars; neutral grey #C5C7C9 for index bars. Slide title about 3.1% of slide height, subtitle about 2.4%, panel headings about 2.0%, chart labels and body about 1.7%, footnote about 1.4%. Thin dark rules (about 0.1% height) under the title and above the footer; thin vertical grey-black dividers between panels. Bars are flat with no corner radius, no shadows, no gradients.

**Layout**

Single-column slide with the title top-left at about 8.8% from the top, a thin dark horizontal rule beneath it spanning the content width, and a subtitle question below the rule. One wide chart fills the central band, with a centred chart title above it and a legend centred below the x-axis; the source footnote sits along the bottom left above the J.P.Morgan Asset Management logo at bottom right, and the page number 27 sits bottom left at the margin.

**Visuals**

Chart: filled area chart titled Stocks with Dividend Yield >=2% Over Time. Y axis titled % of Russell 1000 Value Index, range 30% to 80% with labelled ticks at 30%, 40%, 50%, 60%, 70%, 80%. X axis runs 2004 to about 2025 with labelled ticks at 2004, 2008, 2012, 2016, 2020, 2024. Series 1, Dividend Yield >=2%, is a solid blue area filled from the 30% baseline up to the monthly line; it peaks at about 78% around 2008, about 73% around 2015 and 2019, and bottoms at about 40% around 2020 and about 41% around 2024 (values are estimates, the slide prints none). Series 2, LT Average, is a horizontal gold line at 57%, labelled LT Average = 57% in gold bold text placed inside the blue area around 2017 to 2019. No points are individually highlighted beyond the labelled average. Legend below the x axis: blue square labelled Dividend Yield >=2% and gold line labelled LT Average. Logo: J.P.Morgan wordmark in a serif typeface with ASSET MANAGEMENT in small caps sans-serif below it, black, at bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient, texture or imagery. Titles and body text are black #000000 in Arial (Helvetica-style grotesque sans-serif), title in Arial Bold at about 3.1% of slide height, subtitle and axis labels in regular Arial at about 2.2% to 2.4%, footnote at about 1.7%. Accent colours: chart blue #0092F9 for the area series, gold #FFBE37 for the LT Average line and its label. A thin dark grey rule #3A3A3A about 0.1% high sits under the title. The chart has no gridlines, no borders and no shadows; the x axis baseline is a light grey #BFBFBF hairline.

### Slide 28

**Text**

While historically 57% of the available universe has hit the 2% dividend yield / requirement, the quality of the opportunity set has deteriorated / BARRA Factor Exposures / Profit Exposure / Leverage Exposure / More profitable / LT Average / Less profitable / More leverage / LT Average / Less leverage / >=2% Yield / <2% Yield / >=2% Yield / <2% Yield / Source: JPM Asset Management, Factset, MSCI Barra. Data as of 6/30/2025. There is no guarantee that companies that can issue dividends will declare, continue to / pay, or increase dividends. See Glossary for index of investment terms. / J.P.Morgan / ASSET MANAGEMENT / 28

**Layout**

Two side-by-side line charts in equal columns (left panel x 4 to 46%, right panel x 52 to 94%) under a two-line bold title at the top left with a full-width thin horizontal rule beneath it and a subtitle below the rule. Chart headings are centred above each panel, a shared legend row sits under each panel, a source footnote spans the bottom left above a thin rule, and the J.P.Morgan wordmark with ASSET MANAGEMENT sits bottom right with the slide number bottom left; left margin about 2.8%, right margin about 3%.

**Visuals**

Two line charts, both with the same structure. Left chart: 'Profit Exposure', line chart with no printed y-axis tick labels, vertical axis line at the left edge of the plot and a horizontal x-axis line at the bottom; x-axis years 2004 to 2024 printed every two years at 45-degree rotation. Two series: blue '>=2% Yield' line and yellow '<2% Yield' line. No values are printed; approximate shape: blue about 2004 near the LT Average line, rising to a peak about 2010 above the line, falling below the line from about 2019 and reaching the lowest point about 2024 well below the line; yellow peaks about 2009 above the line, dips below the line about 2010 to 2011, sits near or slightly below the line from 2013 to 2019, and rises above the line about 2021 before ending near the line. A solid black horizontal line marks LT Average across the plot at about the middle of the chart. Two grey arrows with a 1.5 pt stroke point up (labelled More profitable, above the average line) and down (labelled Less profitable, below it) beside the plot. Right chart: 'Leverage Exposure', same format with the same year labels. Blue '>=2% Yield' starts high about 2004, peaks about 2007 and dips about 2009, sits below the line about 2013 to 2018, rises to a peak about 2021 and climbs to its highest point at the right edge in 2025. Yellow '<2% Yield' starts near the line, peaks about 2009, falls to a low about 2009 to 2010 and 2015 area, sits below the line from 2018 onwards, and dips to a trough about 2022 before ending lower. A black horizontal LT Average line sits at about the middle; grey up arrow labelled More leverage and grey down arrow labelled Less leverage sit beside the plot. No highlighted points or annotations other than these labels. Legend under each chart: short blue line sample then '>=2% Yield', short yellow line sample then '<2% Yield'. No photographs, icons or illustrations.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with no texture. Title and chart headings in black #000000, bold Arial (Helvetica-style grotesque), title cap height about 3.1% of slide height (font size about 3.09% of height). Body and labels in black #000000 regular Arial, about 2.2% of slide height; italic Arial for annotations at about 1.84%; footnote Arial about 1.67%; slide number bold Arial about 1.38%. Data series in bright blue #0A8CF0 and golden yellow #FDB933. Annotation arrows in mid grey #808080. Horizontal rule under the title is dark grey #404040 about 0.1% thick spanning the full content width; a light grey rule #BFBFBF sits above the footer. Axis lines black #000000, about 0.2% thick; LT Average lines black, about 0.3% thick. Series lines about 0.4% thick with round joins. No shadows, gradients, corner radii or boxes.

### Slide 29

**Text**

Staying disciplined
We are committed to a disciplined mandate, aiming to provide clients with consistent strong total and risk-adjusted results
Business as Usual:
Investment Philosophy
QVD: Own high-Quality companies at reasonable Valuations that pay healthy and sustainable Dividends
Beta profile
0.8 – 0.9
Number of holdings
85 – 110
Up / Down Capture
Upside participation (~90%), with strong downside protection (~80%) since inception1
Dividend yield
~2.5% average over last decade2
Outcome
Smooth out the ride: A high quality portfolio with less volatility
Additional Flexibility
• Our focus remains on dividend-paying stocks, but there is no minimum dividend yield required at initiation
For illustrative purposes only. The manager seeks to achieve the stated objectives. There can be no guarantee the objectives will be met. Source: J.P. Morgan Asset Management, Morningstar. *PM team Inception: 12/31/2004. Past performance is no guarantee of future results. There is no guarantee that companies that can issue dividends will declare, continue to pay, or increase dividends. See Glossary for index of investment terms.
1 12/31/2004-8/31/2025. 2 Gross portfolio yield, calculated as of August 31, 2025
29
J.P.Morgan
ASSET MANAGEMENT

**Layout**

Single-column slide with a bold title at top left above a thin dark rule spanning the content width, then a one-line subtitle beneath. The middle band is a two-column table: a solid blue block at left spanning the six rows, then a blue label column and a black value column separated by a dashed blue vertical divider. A full-width dashed yellow box sits below the table with a solid yellow label block at left. Footnotes sit at the bottom left above the slide number, with the J.P. Morgan logo at bottom right; left margin about 2.9% and text is left-aligned throughout.

**Visuals**

Two solid blue rectangles (#0092F9) form one tall block at left: upper block x5.2 y21.7 w18.0 h38.0 and lower block x5.2 y59.8 w18.0 h7.0, with a faint hairline seam between them at about y59.8; together they read as one block labelled Business as Usual: in white. Six rows of text, each with a blue bold label and black value, separated by a dashed blue vertical line (about x44.6, y21.7 to y83.7, dash stroke about 0.5% of slide width, #0092F9). A solid yellow rectangle (#FFBE37) x5.2 y71.9 w17.8 h12.0 holds the Additional Flexibility label, and a dashed yellow outline (#FFBE37, about 0.4% stroke, dashes about 2% long) surrounds the whole box x5.0 y71.9 w90.0 h12.0 with the yellow label block inside its left end. A thin dark grey rule (#404040) runs under the title at about y13.5, and a thin grey rule (#808080) runs above the footnotes at about y86.8. The J.P. Morgan logo is a serif wordmark with the words ASSET MANAGEMENT in sans capitals beneath, in black.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with no gradient or texture. Title is Arial Bold (sans-serif, bold) in black #000000 at about 3.0% of slide height, with the subtitle in Arial regular black #000000 at about 2.3%. Row labels are Arial Bold in blue #0092F9 at about 2.2%; values are Arial regular in black #000000 (QVD: and 0.8 – 0.9 and 85 – 110 values in near-black #0D0D0D), with bold words inside the QVD sentence. Block text is Arial Bold white #FFFFFF at about 3.0%. Yellow label is Arial Bold black at about 2.6%. Footnotes are Arial at about 1.5% with bold and bold-italic runs. Page number is Arial Bold at about 1.4%. Shapes have square corners, no shadows, no rounded edges.

### Slide 30

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Splits cumulative performance into three labeled market-period panels, each with the same three bars, so the period-by-period comparison is easy to follow.
- Review: Weaker spots hurt use: slide 30 packs three charts, a table and a line chart into one page with a small axis scale and unlabeled bar groupings, slide 20 is a very small two-table page, and the contents page, the cover and divider slides are plain, so the deck reads as competent compliance-driven material rather than a crafted story.
- Suggested repair: Cut slide 30 into two slides, enlarge the table and chart text on slides 20, 21 and 22, and shorten or shrink the footnote blocks that crowd slides 13 and 30.
- What would change the score: I would add a short summary of the key results up front, trim the pages in the middle that repeat positioning data, and label the colored bars on slide 13 and slide 30 directly so readers do not rely on the legends.

**Layout**

Full-width white slide with the title Equity Income: Setting performance expectations left-aligned at the top, a thin dark rule beneath it, and a three-panel top band (grey bar 1 spanning the full width, then three equal-height boxed panels) above a bottom row split into a left table (under grey bar 2) and a right line chart (under grey bar 3). Footnotes sit in the bottom strip at small size, with the J.P.Morgan Asset Management logo bottom right and page number 30 bottom left, all within margins of about 3 percent.

**Visuals**

Three bar-chart panels with dashed orange borders. Panel 1 (left, S&P 500 below 10%): clustered bar chart with three bars drawn below a horizontal axis line, green Equity Income -18.6%, purple Russell 1000 Value -24.3%, orange-yellow S&P 500 Index -24.2%, and the caption +5.7% Outperformance in olive green #7DA12E. Panel 2 (middle, S&P 500 from (10%) to 20%, no border): three upward bars above a horizontal axis, green Equity Income 8.4%, purple Russell 1000 Value 7.2%, orange-yellow S&P 500 Index 9.7%, caption +1.2% Outperformance in olive green. Panel 3 (right, S&P 500 above 20%): three upward bars above an axis, green Equity Income 23.9%, purple Russell 1000 Value 26.0%, orange-yellow S&P 500 Index 28.8%, caption -2.1% Underperformance in red #FF0000. The black arrow between the panel 2 header words is a plain horizontal arrow with a solid head. Table: header row blue #0092F9 with white bold text, columns Cumulative performance (%)*, 2020 – 2022, 5 QTRs 4Q22 – 1Q24, 6 QTRs 1Q24– 3Q25; row JPMorgan Equity Income Fund (I) with values 27.21, 12.46, 17.33; row Russell 1000 Value Index with values 18.96, 21.48, 17.17; row Excess Returns (bold) with values 8.25 green #00B050, -9.02 red #FF0000, 0.16 green #00A16D. Line chart titled 3) Factor headwinds** in a light grey #F2F2F2 plot area: y axis Percent from -40% to 10% in 10% steps with gridlines, x axis Jan-23, Jul-23, Jan-24, Jul-24, Jan-25 (monthly data, dates from Jan-23 to Jan-25). Series: High Quality (ROE) in yellow-orange #FFBE37 ending about -8% (label -8%), starting near 0% and mostly between -5% and -12%; Low Volatility in purple #6F44BB ending -33% (label -33%), falling from about 0% to about -34% near Jan-24, then near -30% and about -33% at the end; Dividend Yield in green #00A16D ending -10% (label -10%), fluctuating roughly between 0% and -15%. Legend below the plot: thick short line segments with labels High Quality (ROE), Low Volatility, Dividend Yield. A black horizontal line marks 0%. Highlighted points: end-of-series labels -8%, -10%, -33%.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate white design. Title in Arial Bold black #000000, large. Section header bars in mid-grey #7F7F7F with white Arial Bold text. Subtle panel labels in very light grey #F5F7F8. Body and table text in Arial regular #000000 or near-black #101417. Accents: olive green #7DA12E for outperformance captions, red #FF0000 for underperformance, orange-yellow #FFBE37 for dashed panel borders and series, green #00A16D and purple #6F44BB for chart series, blue #0092F9 table header. Title is about 3.3 percent of slide height, body text about 2.0 percent, footnotes about 1.5 percent. Panel borders are dashed, about 0.4 percent stroke width, with square corners and no shadows. Table rows separated by thin light grey rules.

### Slide 31

**Text**

A long track record of consistent outperformance / JPMorgan Equity Income Portfolio as of September 30, 2025 / JPMorgan Equity Income Fund: 5 Year and 10 Year Rolling Excess Returns vs. Russell 1000 Value Index / 5 Year Rolling Excess Return / 10 Year Rolling Excess Return / 5% / 4% / 3% / 2% / 1% / 0% / -1% / -2% / Equity Income has outperformed: / 83% of rolling 5-year periods / 100% of rolling 10-year periods / Jul-10 / Feb-11 / Sep-11 / Apr-12 / Nov-12 / Jun-13 / Jan-14 / Aug-14 / Mar-15 / Oct-15 / May-16 / Dec-16 / Jul-17 / Feb-18 / Sep-18 / Apr-19 / Nov-19 / Jun-20 / Jan-21 / Aug-21 / Mar-22 / Oct-22 / May-23 / Dec-23 / Jul-24 / Feb-25 / Sep-25 / Past performance is no guarantee of future results. / Fund start date: 7/12/1987, ¹Inception: 12/31/2004. Rolling excess related returns (net of fees) since PM inception for the for JPMorgan Equity Income Fund (HLIEX) are against the Russell 1000 Value Index. / Indices do not include fees or operating expenses and are not available for actual investment. The related performance calculations are shown for illustrated purposes only and are not meant to be / representative of actual results achieved by the manager while investing in the respective strategies over the time periods shown. The related performance results have certain inherent limitations. These / related performance results do not take into consideration the ongoing implementation of the manager’s proprietary investment strategies. No representation is being made that any portfolio will or is likely to / achieve profits or losses similar to those shown. Returns will fluctuate and an investment upon redemption may be worth more or less than its original value. See Glossary for index of investment terms. / J.P.Morgan / ASSET MANAGEMENT / 31

**Layout**

Single-column slide with a left-aligned title at the top (x 2.8%, y 9.6%), a thin black rule beneath it, and a left-aligned subtitle below; a full-width blue header bar carries the chart title, followed by one full-width chart with the legend centred above the plot. Footnotes sit left-aligned across the bottom at a 2.8% left margin, with the J.P.Morgan wordmark and ASSET MANAGEMENT tagline at bottom right and the page number '31' at bottom left.

**Visuals**

One chart: a monthly clustered column chart (vertical bars) titled in the blue header bar as JPMorgan Equity Income Fund: 5 Year and 10 Year Rolling Excess Returns vs. Russell 1000 Value Index. Y-axis: excess return in percent, labelled 5%, 4%, 3%, 2%, 1%, 0%, -1%, -2%, gridlines at 1% steps in light grey, range -2% to 5%. X-axis: time, monthly from Jul-10 to Sep-25, labels every 6 months (Jul-10, Feb-11, Sep-11, Apr-12, Nov-12, Jun-13, Jan-14, Aug-14, Mar-15, Oct-15, May-16, Dec-16, Jul-17, Feb-18, Sep-18, Apr-19, Nov-19, Jun-20, Jan-21, Aug-21, Mar-22, Oct-22, May-23, Dec-23, Jul-24, Feb-25, Sep-25), rotated vertical. Series 1: 5 Year Rolling Excess Return, blue bars #0092F9; about 2.1% in Jul-10, rising to about 4.1% around Sep-11 to Apr-12, falling to about 1.0% by Jan-14, dipping to about -0.8% in early 2014, mostly 0% to 2% from 2015, troughing about -1.0% around Dec-16 and again about -1.05% at Sep-25. Series 2: 10 Year Rolling Excess Return, gold bars #FFBE37, beginning about early 2015 at about 1.6%, peaking about 2.3% around late 2015, about 1.2% to 2.1% through 2018 to 2020, about 0.5% to 1.5% in 2021 to 2022, and about 0.5% to 1.0% in 2023 to 2025. Legend: centred above the plot, blue square 5 Year Rolling Excess Return, gold square 10 Year Rolling Excess Return. Callout box at upper right of plot: black 1 px border, white fill, text Equity Income has outperformed: (bold, underlined) then 83% of rolling 5-year periods and 100% of rolling 10-year periods. No highlighted data points.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide in Arial-style sans-serif. Title Arial Bold, black #000000, about 3.1% of slide height, sentence case. Subtitle Arial regular, black #000000, about 2.4%. Header bar text Arial Bold white #FFFFFF about 2.0%. Chart tick labels Arial regular grey #595959 about 1.8%. Legend and callout text Arial regular black #000000 about 1.7% to 1.8%; callout heading bold dark slate #21292E underlined. Footnotes Arial regular black #000000 about 1.4%, fund start date line in #373741. Thin black horizontal rule under title about 0.15% high. Blue header bar #0092F9 with no border, square corners. Callout box white with 1 px black outline, square corners. Gridlines light grey hairline. Zero line light grey. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 32

**Text**

Dividends have been an important contributor to total returns over time / As of September 30, 2025 / Total Return Decomposition – Ibbotson Associates SBBI U.S. Large Company Stocks Index / Dividends / Capital Appreciation / 13.9% / 4.7% / 13.6% / 5.1% / 12.6% / 4.4% / 15.3% / 2.5% / 1.8% / -2.7% / 11.2% / 2.1% / 13.5% / 1.6% / 6.5% / 3.8% / 3.0% / 6.0% / 4.4% / 1.6% / 3.3% / 4.2% / 5.4% / -5.3% / 1927-1929 / 1930s / 1940s / 1950s / 1960s / 1970s / 1980s / 1990s / 2000s / 2010s / 2020s / 1927 - Present / Past performance is not a guarantee for future results. Source: Morningstar. Shown for illustrative purposes only. There is no guarantee that companies will declare, continue to pay or increase dividends. / See Glossary for index of investment terms. / 32 / J.P.Morgan / ASSET MANAGEMENT

**Layout**

Single full-width slide with a left-aligned title block at the top (title, rule, subtitle, chart heading) and a full-width vertical stacked column chart occupying the middle band. A thin rule and the footnote sit near the bottom, with the J.P.Morgan wordmark bottom-right and the page number bottom-left; left margin about 2.8 percent and right margin about 2.7 percent of slide width.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked column chart (two stacked series per column, positive and negative values stacked from a zero baseline). Title: none inside the chart; the heading above it reads Total Return Decomposition – Ibbotson Associates SBBI U.S. Large Company Stocks Index. Categories left to right: 1927-1929, 1930s, 1940s, 1950s, 1960s, 1970s, 1980s, 1990s, 2000s, 2010s, 2020s, 1927 - Present. Series Dividends (blue #0092F9, bottom segment): 1927-1929 4.7%; 1930s 5.4%; 1940s 6.0%; 1950s 5.1%; 1960s 3.3%; 1970s 4.2%; 1980s 4.4%; 1990s 2.5%; 2000s 1.8%; 2010s 2.1%; 2020s 1.6%; 1927 - Present 3.8%. Series Capital Appreciation (amber #FFBE37, top segment, negative values below zero): 1927-1929 13.9%; 1930s -5.3%; 1940s 3.0%; 1950s 13.6%; 1960s 4.4%; 1970s 1.6%; 1980s 12.6%; 1990s 15.3%; 2000s -2.7%; 2010s 11.2%; 2020s 13.5%; 1927 - Present 6.5%. Value axis: no numeric tick labels are printed; gridlines at about 5 percent intervals, from about -10 percent (bottom gridline) to about 20 percent (top gridline), zero line solid black. Category axis: no tick marks, labels printed beneath the plot. Legend: top right inside the plot area, horizontal, Dividends with blue square then Capital Appreciation with amber square. Highlighted points: none. Values printed inside each segment, centred.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate report style on white. Titles bold black Arial-style sans-serif (Arial Bold), body and chart labels regular Arial-style sans-serif in black #000000. Title about 3.1 percent of slide height, subtitle about 2.4 percent, chart heading about 2.0 percent, chart labels about 1.8 percent, footnote about 1.4 percent. Accents: blue #0092F9 and amber #FFBE37 for the two series. Title rule is a thin dark grey #333333 line; footer rule is a thin light grey #B0B0B0 line. Gridlines are thin light grey #C8C8C8; zero baseline is a solid black line about 2 px. Bars are flat, square-cornered, no outlines, no shadows, no gradients.
